# Workers Best Practices — Rules

Each rule has an imperative summary, what to check, the correct pattern, and an anti-pattern where applicable. Code examples are plain TypeScript — no MDX components.

When a rule involves config fields or API signatures that may evolve, a **Retrieve** callout reminds you to check the latest docs or types before flagging. All doc paths are relative to `https://developers.cloudflare.com`.

---

## Configuration

### Keep compatibility_date current

Set `compatibility_date` to today on new projects. Update periodically on existing ones to access new APIs and fixes.

**Check**: `compatibility_date` exists. Flag if older than 6 months.

```jsonc
// wrangler.jsonc
{
  "compatibility_date": "$today",  // Replace with today's date (YYYY-MM-DD)
  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"]
}
```

**Retrieve**: current compatibility dates at `/workers/configuration/compatibility-dates/`.

### Enable nodejs_compat

The `nodejs_compat` flag enables Node.js built-in modules (`node:crypto`, `node:buffer`, `node:stream`). Many libraries require it. Missing this flag causes cryptic import errors at runtime.

**Check**: `compatibility_flags` includes `"nodejs_compat"`.

```jsonc
{
  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"]
}
```

### Generate binding types with wrangler types

Never hand-write the `Env` interface. Run `wrangler types` to generate it from the wrangler config. Re-run after adding or renaming any binding.

**Check**: no manually defined `Env` or `interface Env` that duplicates wrangler config bindings. Look for `satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>` pattern on the default export.

```ts
// Generated by wrangler types — always matches actual config
export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    const value = await env.MY_KV.get("key");
    return new Response(value);
  },
} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Hand-written Env that drifts from actual bindings
interface Env {
  MY_KV: KVNamespace;  // What if the binding name changed?
}
```

### Store secrets with wrangler secret

Secrets must never appear in wrangler config or source code. Use `wrangler secret put` and access via `env` at runtime. Non-secret config goes in `vars`.

**Check**: no string literals that look like API keys, tokens, or credentials. Verify `.env` is in `.gitignore` for local dev.

```jsonc
{
  "vars": {
    "API_BASE_URL": "https://api.example.com"  // Non-secret: OK in config
  }
  // Secrets set via: wrangler secret put API_KEY
}
```

Anti-pattern:
```jsonc
{
  "vars": {
    "API_KEY": "sk-live-abc123..."  // Secret in version control
  }
}
```

### Use wrangler.jsonc for config

Prefer `wrangler.jsonc` over `wrangler.toml`. Newer features are JSON-only. JSONC supports comments for documenting config decisions.

**Check**: project uses `wrangler.jsonc` (or `wrangler.json`). Flag `wrangler.toml` in new projects.

---

## Request & Response Handling

### Stream request and response bodies

Workers have a 128 MB memory limit. Buffering entire bodies with `await response.text()` or `await request.arrayBuffer()` crashes on large payloads. Stream data through using `TransformStream` or pass `response.body` directly.

**Check**: any `await response.text()`, `await response.json()`, or `await response.arrayBuffer()` on data that could be large or unbounded. Small, bounded payloads (known-size JSON, config files) are fine to buffer.

Correct — stream through:
```ts
async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/large-dataset");
  return new Response(response.body, response);
}
```

Correct — concatenate multiple streams:
```ts
async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
  const urls = ["https://api.example.com/part-1", "https://api.example.com/part-2"];
  const { readable, writable } = new TransformStream();

  // Track the pipeline promise — don't let it float
  ctx.waitUntil((async () => {
    for (const url of urls) {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (response.body) {
        await response.body.pipeTo(writable, { preventClose: true });
      }
    }
    await writable.close();
  })());

  return new Response(readable, {
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream" },
  });
}
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Buffers entire body — crashes on large payloads
const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/large-dataset");
const text = await response.text();
return new Response(text);
```

**Retrieve**: streaming APIs at `/workers/runtime-apis/streams/`.

### Use waitUntil for work after the response

`ctx.waitUntil()` performs background work (analytics, cache writes, webhooks) after the response is sent. Keeps response fast. 30-second time limit after response.

**Check**: background work uses `ctx.waitUntil()`, not inline `await`. Do not destructure `ctx` — it loses the `this` binding and throws "Illegal invocation".

```ts
async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
  const data = await processRequest(request);

  ctx.waitUntil(logToAnalytics(env, data));
  ctx.waitUntil(updateCache(env, data));

  return Response.json(data);
}
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Destructuring ctx loses the this binding
const { waitUntil } = ctx;  // "Illegal invocation" at runtime
waitUntil(somePromise);
```

---

## Architecture

### Use bindings for Cloudflare services, not REST APIs

Bindings (KV, R2, D1, Queues, Workflows) are direct, in-process references — no network hop, no authentication, no extra latency. Using the Cloudflare REST API from a Worker wastes time and adds complexity.

**Check**: no `fetch("https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/...")` calls for services available as bindings.

```ts
// Binding — direct, zero-cost
const object = await env.MY_BUCKET.get("my-file");
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// REST API from inside a Worker — unnecessary overhead
const response = await fetch(
  "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/.../r2/buckets/.../objects/my-file",
  { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.CF_API_TOKEN}` } }
);
```

### Use Queues and Workflows for async and background work

Long-running, retriable, or non-urgent tasks should not block a request.

- **Queues**: decouple producer from consumer. Fan-out, buffering/batching, simple single-step background jobs. At-least-once delivery.
- **Workflows**: multi-step durable execution. Each step's return value is persisted; only failed steps retry. Can run for hours/days/weeks.
- **Both together**: Queue buffers high-throughput entry, consumer creates Workflow instances for complex processing.

**Check**: long-running work (email sends, webhooks, multi-step processes) is offloaded to Queues or Workflows, not done inline in the fetch handler.

```ts
async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const order = await request.json<{ id: string; type: string }>();

  if (order.type === "simple") {
    await env.ORDER_QUEUE.send({ orderId: order.id, action: "send-email" });
  } else {
    await env.FULFILLMENT_WORKFLOW.create({ params: { orderId: order.id } });
  }

  return Response.json({ status: "accepted" }, { status: 202 });
}
```

**Retrieve**: `/queues/` and `/workflows/` for current APIs. For Workflow-specific rules, see [Rules of Workflows](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workflows/build/rules-of-workflows/).

### Use service bindings for Worker-to-Worker communication

Service bindings are zero-cost, bypass the public internet, and support type-safe RPC. Do not call another Worker via its public URL.

**Check**: Worker-to-Worker calls use `env.SERVICE_NAME.method()` (RPC) or `env.SERVICE_NAME.fetch()`, not `fetch("https://my-other-worker.example.com/...")`.

```ts
import { WorkerEntrypoint } from "cloudflare:workers";

export class AuthService extends WorkerEntrypoint {
  async verifyToken(token: string): Promise<{ userId: string; valid: boolean }> {
    return { userId: "user-123", valid: true };
  }
}

// Caller Worker
const auth = await env.AUTH_SERVICE.verifyToken(token);
```

**Retrieve**: verify `WorkerEntrypoint` import path and signature against latest `@cloudflare/workers-types`.

### Use Hyperdrive for external database connections

Hyperdrive maintains a regional connection pool, eliminating per-request TCP + TLS + auth cost (often 300-500ms). Create a new `Client` per request — Hyperdrive manages the underlying pool. Requires `nodejs_compat`.

**Check**: any `new Client()` or database connection that uses a direct connection string instead of `env.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString`.

```jsonc
{
  "hyperdrive": [{ "binding": "HYPERDRIVE", "id": "<YOUR_HYPERDRIVE_ID>" }]
}
```

```ts
import { Client } from "pg";

async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
  const client = new Client({ connectionString: env.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString });
  await client.connect();
  const result = await client.query("SELECT id, name FROM users LIMIT 10");
  return Response.json(result.rows);
}
```

**Retrieve**: `/hyperdrive/` for current configuration and supported databases.

---

## Observability

### Enable Workers Logs and Traces

Enable `observability` in wrangler config before deploying to production. Use `head_sampling_rate` to control volume and cost. Use structured JSON logging — `console.log(JSON.stringify({...}))` — so logs are searchable. Use `console.error` for errors (appears at error severity in the dashboard).

**Check**: `observability.enabled` is `true` in config. Logging uses structured JSON, not string concatenation.

```jsonc
{
  "observability": {
    "enabled": true,
    "logs": { "head_sampling_rate": 1 },
    "traces": { "enabled": true, "head_sampling_rate": 0.01 }
  }
}
```

```ts
// Structured JSON — searchable and filterable
console.log(JSON.stringify({ message: "incoming request", method: request.method, path: url.pathname }));

// Error severity
console.error(JSON.stringify({ message: "request failed", error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }));
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Unstructured string logs — hard to query
console.log("Got a request to " + url.pathname);
```

**Retrieve**: `/workers/observability/logs/workers-logs/` and `/workers/observability/traces/` for current config options.

---

## Code Patterns

### Do not store request-scoped state in global scope

Workers reuse isolates across requests. Module-level mutable variables cause cross-request data leaks, stale state, and "Cannot perform I/O on behalf of a different request" errors.

**Check**: no mutable `let`/`var` at module scope that gets assigned inside a handler. Pass state through function arguments.

```ts
export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
    const userId = request.headers.get("X-User-Id");
    const result = await handleRequest(userId, env);
    return Response.json(result);
  },
} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Module-level mutable state — leaks between requests
let currentUser: string | null = null;

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    currentUser = request.headers.get("X-User-Id");  // Visible to next request
    // ...
  },
};
```

### Always await or waitUntil Promises

A Promise that is not `await`ed, `return`ed, or passed to `ctx.waitUntil()` is a floating promise. Causes: dropped results, swallowed errors, unfinished work. The runtime may terminate the isolate before it completes.

**Check**: every `fetch()`, `env.*.put()`, `env.*.send()`, and any other async call is handled. Enable `no-floating-promises` lint rule.

```bash
# ESLint
npx eslint --rule '{"@typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises": "error"}' src/

# oxlint
npx oxlint --deny typescript/no-floating-promises src/
```

```ts
// Correct: await when you need the result
const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/process", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) });

// Correct: waitUntil when you don't need the result before responding
ctx.waitUntil(fetch("https://api.example.com/webhook", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) }));
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Floating promise — result dropped, error swallowed
fetch("https://api.example.com/webhook", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) });
```

### Be aware of platform limits

Workers have a 10ms CPU time limit (Bundled) or 30s (Standard/Unbound). Heavy synchronous work — tight loops, large JSON parsing, compute-intensive crypto — can hit the CPU limit and terminate the request.

**Check**: compute-heavy operations that run synchronously. Consider breaking work into smaller chunks, offloading to Queues/Workflows, or using WebAssembly for CPU-intensive tasks.

**Retrieve**: current limits at `/workers/platform/limits/`.

---

## Security

### Use Web Crypto for secure token generation

Use `crypto.randomUUID()` for unique IDs and `crypto.getRandomValues()` for random bytes. `Math.random()` is not cryptographically secure.

For comparing secrets (API keys, HMAC signatures), use `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual()`. Hash both values to a fixed size first — do not short-circuit on length mismatch (leaks length via timing).

**Check**: no `Math.random()` for security-sensitive values. Secret comparisons use `timingSafeEqual` with fixed-size hashing.

```ts
// Secure random UUID
const sessionId = crypto.randomUUID();

// Secure random bytes
const tokenBytes = new Uint8Array(32);
crypto.getRandomValues(tokenBytes);
const token = Array.from(tokenBytes).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
```

```ts
// Constant-time comparison — hash first to avoid length leak
async function verifyToken(provided: string, expected: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const [providedHash, expectedHash] = await Promise.all([
    crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoder.encode(provided)),
    crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoder.encode(expected)),
  ]);
  return crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(providedHash, expectedHash);
}
```

Anti-pattern:
```ts
// Predictable — not cryptographically secure
const token = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2);

// Timing side-channel — leaks information about the expected value
return provided === expected;
```

**Retrieve**: `/workers/runtime-apis/web-crypto/` for current API surface.

### Explicit error handling over passThroughOnException

`passThroughOnException()` is a fail-open mechanism that sends requests to the origin when the Worker throws. It hides bugs and makes debugging difficult. Use explicit try/catch with structured error responses.

**Check**: no `ctx.passThroughOnException()` calls. Error handling uses try/catch with structured JSON error responses and `console.error`.

```ts
async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
  try {
    const result = await handleRequest(request, env);
    return Response.json(result);
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error";
    console.error(JSON.stringify({ message: "unhandled error", error: message, path: new URL(request.url).pathname }));
    return Response.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
```

---

## Development & Testing

### Test with @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers

Runs tests inside the Workers runtime with real bindings. Catches issues that Node.js-based tests miss.

**Known pitfall**: the Vitest pool auto-injects `nodejs_compat`, so tests pass even if your wrangler config is missing the flag. Always confirm your `wrangler.jsonc` includes `nodejs_compat` if your code depends on Node.js built-ins.

**Check**: test setup uses `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers`. Tests cover nullable returns (e.g., KV `.get()` returning `null`).

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { env } from "cloudflare:test";

describe("KV operations", () => {
  it("should store and retrieve a value", async () => {
    await env.MY_KV.put("key", "value");
    const result = await env.MY_KV.get("key");
    expect(result).toBe("value");
  });

  it("should return null for missing keys", async () => {
    const result = await env.MY_KV.get("nonexistent");
    expect(result).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

**Retrieve**: `/workers/testing/vitest-integration/` for current setup and configuration.

FILE: C:\Users\ivych\.codex\skills\workers-best-practices\references\review.md
# Code Review — Workers

How to review Workers code for type correctness, API usage, config validity, and best practices. This is self-contained — do not assume access to other skills.

## Retrieval

Prefer retrieval over pre-training. Types, config schemas, and APIs change with compatibility dates and new bindings.

### Workers types

Fetch the latest `@cloudflare/workers-types` before reviewing. The project may have an older version installed.

```bash
mkdir -p /tmp/workers-types-latest && \
  npm pack @cloudflare/workers-types --pack-destination /tmp/workers-types-latest && \
  tar -xzf /tmp/workers-types-latest/cloudflare-workers-types-*.tgz -C /tmp/workers-types-latest
# Types are at /tmp/workers-types-latest/package/index.d.ts
```

Search this file for the specific type, class, or interface under review. Do not guess type names.

Alternative: `npx wrangler types` generates a typed `Env` interface from the local wrangler config.

Fallback: read `node_modules/@cloudflare/workers-types/index.d.ts`. Note the installed version.

### Wrangler config schema

The authoritative schema is bundled with wrangler as `config-schema.json` (JSON Schema draft-07).

```bash
# Read from local node_modules
cat node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json
```

Do not guess field names or structures — look them up.

### Cloudflare docs

Use the Cloudflare docs search tool if available, or fetch from `https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/`. The best practices page lives at `/workers/best-practices/workers-best-practices/`.

---

## Type Validation

### Env interface

- Every binding must have a specific type. Flag `any`, `unknown`, `object`, or `Record<string, unknown>` on bindings.
- Binding types that accept generic parameters (Durable Object namespaces, Queues, Service bindings for RPC) must include them. Read the type definition to confirm which types are generic.
- Binding names must match the wrangler config exactly.
- Prefer generated types from `wrangler types` over hand-written interfaces.

### Handler and class signatures

Verify against current type definitions — do not assume signatures are stable.

- Correct import path (most Workers platform classes import from `"cloudflare:workers"`)
- Generic type parameter on base classes (e.g., `DurableObject<Env>`)
- Binding access pattern: `env.X` in module export handlers, `this.env.X` in classes extending platform base classes
- `ExecutionContext` as the third param in module export handlers (needed for `ctx.waitUntil()`)
- `fetch()` handlers must return `Promise<Response>`

### Binding access — the most common error

- **Module export handlers** (`fetch`, `scheduled`, `queue`, `email`): bindings via `env.X` parameter
- **Platform base classes** (`WorkerEntrypoint`, `DurableObject`, `Workflow`, `Agent`): bindings via `this.env.X`

Flag `env.X` inside a class extending a platform base class. Flag `this.env.X` inside a module export handler.

### Type integrity rules

| Rule | Detail |
|------|--------|
| No `any` | Never on binding types, handler params, or API responses |
| No double-casting | `as unknown as T` hides real incompatibilities — fix the underlying design |
| Justify suppressions | `@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error` must include a comment explaining why |
| Prefer `satisfies` | Use `satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>` over `as` — validates without widening |
| Validate, do not assert | Schema or type guard for untyped data (JSON, parsed bodies), not `as` |

### Stale class patterns

Old patterns survive in codebases long after APIs change.

- **`extends` vs `implements`**: platform classes use `extends`, not `implements`. The `implements` pattern is legacy and loses `this.ctx`, `this.env`.
- **Import paths**: verify module specifiers match what types actually export. Common mistake: wrong path for `"cloudflare:workers"` vs `"cloudflare:workflows"`.
- **Renamed properties**: e.g., `this.state` to `this.ctx` in Durable Objects. Search types to confirm.
- **Constructor signatures**: base class constructors change. Verify expected parameters.

---

## Config Validation

### Required fields

For executable examples, verify: `name`, `compatibility_date`, `main`. Check the schema for current required fields.

### Config format

- **JSONC** (`wrangler.jsonc`) — preferred for new projects
- **JSON** (`wrangler.json`) — valid but no comments
- **TOML** (`wrangler.toml`) — legacy; acceptable in existing content, flag in new projects

### Binding-code consistency

1. Every `env.X` reference in code has a corresponding binding declaration in config
2. Every binding in config is referenced in code (warn on unused)
3. Names match exactly (case-sensitive)
4. For Durable Objects: `class_name` matches the exported class name

### Common config mistakes

| Check | What to look for |
|-------|-----------------|
| Stale `compatibility_date` | Should be recent; use `$today` placeholder in docs |
| Missing DO migrations | Every new DO class needs a migration entry |
| Binding name mismatch | Config `binding`/`name` must match `env.X` in code |
| Secrets in config | Never in `vars` — use `wrangler secret put` |
| Wrong binding key | Verify top-level key name against the schema |
| Missing entrypoint | `main` required for executable Workers |

---

## Anti-Patterns to Flag

See the full anti-patterns table in `SKILL.md`. The type-specific ones to watch for during review:

- **`any` on `Env` or handler params** — defeats type safety for all downstream binding access
- **`as unknown as T`** — hides real type incompatibilities; fix the underlying design
- **`@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error` without explanation** — masks errors silently; require a justifying comment
- **`implements` instead of `extends` on platform base classes** — legacy pattern; loses `this.ctx`, `this.env`
- **`env.X` inside class body** — should be `this.env.X` in platform base classes
- **`this.env.X` in module export handler** — should be `env.X` parameter
- **Non-serializable values across boundaries** — `Response`, `Error` in step/queue compiles but fails at runtime

---

## Serialization Boundaries

Data crossing these boundaries must be structured-clone serializable:

- **Queue messages**: body passed to `.send()` or `.sendBatch()`
- **Workflow step return values**: persisted to durable storage
- **DO storage**: values in `storage.put()` or SQL
- **`postMessage()`**: WebSocket messages

Non-serializable types to flag: `Response`, `Request`, `Error`, functions, class instances with methods, `Map`/`Set`, `Symbol`.

Valid: plain objects, arrays, strings, numbers, booleans, null, `ArrayBuffer`, `Date`.

---

## Review Process

1. **Retrieve** — fetch latest workers types, wrangler schema, and best practices page
2. **Read full files** — not just diffs; context matters for binding access patterns
3. **Categorize code** — determines what to check:
   - **Illustrative** (concept demo, comments for most logic): verify correct API names and realistic signatures
   - **Demonstrative** (functional snippet, would work in context): verify syntax, correct APIs, correct binding access
   - **Executable** (standalone, runs without modification): verify compiles, runs, includes imports and config
4. **Check types** — binding access pattern, handler signatures, no `any`, no unsafe casts
5. **Check config** — compatibility_date, nodejs_compat, observability, secrets, binding-code consistency
6. **Check patterns** — streaming, floating promises, global state, serialization boundaries
7. **Check security** — crypto usage, secret handling, timing-safe comparisons, error handling
8. **Validate with tools** — `npx tsc --noEmit`, lint for `no-floating-promises`
9. **Assess risk** — HIGH (auth, crypto, bindings), MEDIUM (business logic, config), LOW (style, comments)

### Output format

```
**[SEVERITY]** Brief description
`file.ts:42` — explanation with evidence
Suggested fix: `code`
```

Severity: **CRITICAL** (security, data loss, crash) | **HIGH** (type error, wrong API, broken config) | **MEDIUM** (missing validation, edge case) | **LOW** (style, minor improvement)

13:
14:## 1. 資料契約(新端點,不改既有端點)
15:
16:`POST /trace`
17:
18:請求:
19:```
20:Headers: X-Line-AccessToken: <token>, Content-Type: application/json
21:Body: { "log_id": "recXXXXXXXXXXXXXX", "trace_text": "<TA 輸入,1-500 字>" }
22:```
23:
24:行為(仿 `/log/seal` 既有模式,S37 紀律):
25:1. `OPTIONS` → 204 + `corsHeaders()`(既有全域 OPTIONS 分支已覆蓋,免另寫)。
26:2. Content-Type 非 json → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
27:3. body parse 失敗 → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
28:4. 缺 `X-Line-AccessToken` → 401 `Missing access token`。
29:5. `resolveUserId` 失敗(token 過期/非本 channel)→ 401 `Invalid access token`。
30:6. payload 白名單:僅允許 `log_id`、`trace_text` 兩鍵,多餘鍵 → 400 `Unexpected field in payload`。
31:7. `log_id` 非 `/^rec[a-zA-Z0-9]{14}$/` → 400 `Invalid log_id`。
32:8. `trace_text`:非字串 / trim 後空字串 / trim 後長度 >500 → 400 `Invalid trace_text`。
33:9. `AIRTABLE_API_KEY` 未配置 → 503(既有模式)。
34:10. GET 該筆 Divination_Log record:
35:    - fetch 失敗(network)→ 502 `Airtable read failed`。
36:    - 404 → 404 `{record:null}`。
37:    - 非 ok 非 404 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
38:    - JSON parse 失敗 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
39:11. **擁有權驗證**:`f.line_user_id_raw !== verifiedUserId` → **403** `Forbidden`(他人 log_id 寫入的機讀證據項,card 驗收 #2/#5)。
40:12. PATCH 該筆 `trace_text`(trim 後)+ `trace_at`(伺服端 `new Date().toISOString()`,+08:00 由 Airtable Date 欄時區顯示設定負責,伺服端一律存 UTC ISO,與 `golden_seal_time` 既有慣例一致)。
41:    - PATCH 失敗(network/非 ok)→ 502 `Airtable write failed`。
42:13. 成功 → 200 `{ traced: true, trace_text: "<trim後>", trace_at: "<ISO>" }`。
43:
44:**覆寫策略(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已修正措辭)**:明確定義為 **last-write-wins**,非「冪等」——重送同一請求會把 `trace_at` 更新為最新一次寫入的時間戳,結果本就會變,這是刻意設計而非缺陷。存取控制仍由 §步驟11 擁有權驗證(僅本人 LINE token 可寫自己的卦記)保證,前端隱藏入口按鈕只是體感引導,不是安全邊界,不作此宣稱。原「客服代補情境」提案已撤回(超出本卡範圍,若未來要開管理端點另立派工卡)。
45:
46:**記錄類型守門(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已採納)**:PATCH 前需再驗證 `(f.entry_type || "divination") !== "divination"` → 404(視為找不到,不寫入非卦記類型如 mood/deepdive/fupan)。
47:
48:**Request body 位元組上限(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已採納)**:改用 `request.text()` 讀 raw body,以 `TextEncoder` 量測位元組數,超過 `TRACE_MAX_BODY_BYTES = 4096` → **413** `Payload too large`,再對 raw text 做 `JSON.parse`(不再直接呼叫 `request.json()`,避免在做 500 字檢查前就無上限吃進整個 body)。
49:
50:**Content-Type 嚴格化(第 1 輪互審 SUGGEST 已採納)**:改用 `/^application\/json(?:;.*)?$/i` 比對整個 header 值,不再用寬鬆 `includes`,避免 `application/jsonp` 之類誤判通過。
51:
52:`/history` 與 `/log` 回傳欄位白名單追加 `trace_text`、`trace_at`(仿現行 `golden_seal`/`golden_seal_time` 追加方式,`airtableFetch` 的 `fields` 陣列與 `/log` 的 `record` 物件組裝處各加兩行)。
53:
54:**第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 答辯(不採納,附理由)**:
55:- 「`resolveUserId` 把 LINE 429/5xx/逾時全部歸類成 401」——屬實,但 `resolveUserId` 是 `/log/seal`、`/trigger/deepdive`、`/trigger/fupan`、`POST /` 四個既有端點共用的函式,此問題在這些端點上早已存在,並非本次新增。§2 明確禁止修改 `resolveUserId`(diff 邊界鐵律,card 也未授權動共用函式)。此為既有系統性缺口,建議另立獨立派工卡統一修,不阻擋本卡(本卡新端點的錯誤分類與既有三個端點完全一致,不製造新的不一致)。`/trace` 的 catch 區塊已改為透傳 `e.status`/`e.message`(不再無條件寫死 401),為未來若修 `resolveUserId` 預留正確傳遞路徑。
56:

60:- `/history` fields 陣列追加 `"trace_text","trace_at"`;`records.map` 組裝物件追加 `trace_text: f.trace_text || null, trace_at: f.trace_at || null`。

FILE tests\test_e25_stamp_v1_0.sh
1:#!/bin/bash
2:# test_e25_stamp_v1_0.sh — E25② 卦記蓋印(補後續)回歸鉤
3:# Ref: plans/plan_e25_stamp_v0_1.md(Codex 互審 r1 10 條 BLOCKER 已回應)
4:# V1(Worker /trace headless 全契約矩陣,Node ESM import 真檔 worker.js;缺檔=FAIL,不可假綠燈)
5:# V2(log.html 文案逐字唯一定義)
6:# V3(log.html stamp 函式真執行,含 XSS escaping 斷言,非純 regex)
7:# 全程假 token / 測試 log_id,不碰真鑰,不打真 Airtable/LINE
8:
9:set -uo pipefail
10:PASS=0; FAIL=0
11:
12:IDX="$(dirname "$0")/../log.html"
13:WORKER="${WORKER_JS_PATH:-$(dirname "$0")/../../mingge-relay/worker.js}"
14:
15:echo "=== V1: Worker /trace headless 全契約矩陣(Node ESM import 真檔) ==="
16:
17:if [ ! -f "$WORKER" ]; then
18:  echo "FAIL - worker.js not found at \$WORKER_JS_PATH ($WORKER) — 必要 artifact 缺失,視為 FAIL(不可假綠燈)"
19:  FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
20:else
21:  NODE_OUT=$(WORKER_PATH="$WORKER" node --input-type=module -e "
22:import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
23:const worker = (await import(pathToFileURL(process.env.WORKER_PATH).href)).default;
24:
25:const OWNER = 'Uowner00000000000000000000000';
26:const OTHER = 'Uother00000000000000000000000';
27:const LOG_ID = 'recAAAAAAAAAAAAAA';
28:
29:function mockFetch(scenario) {
30:  return async (url, opts = {}) => {
31:    const u = String(url);
32:    if (u.includes('line.me/oauth2')) {
33:      if (scenario.lineVerifyDown) throw new Error('network down');
34:      if (scenario.badToken) return { ok: false, status: 401 };
35:      return { ok: true, json: async () => ({ client_id: '2010192384' }) };
36:    }
37:    if (u.includes('line.me/v2/profile')) {
38:      return { ok: true, json: async () => ({ userId: scenario.callerId, displayName: 'TA' }) };
39:    }
40:    if (u.includes('api.airtable.com') && (!opts.method || opts.method === 'GET')) {
41:      if (scenario.airtableGetNetworkDown) throw new Error('network down');
42:      if (scenario.recordMissing) return { ok: false, status: 404 };
43:      if (scenario.airtableGetNonOk) return { ok: false, status: 500 };
44:      if (scenario.airtableGetBadJson) return { ok: true, json: async () => { throw new Error('bad json'); } };
45:      return {
46:        ok: true,
47:        json: async () => ({ id: LOG_ID, fields: { line_user_id_raw: OWNER, entry_type: scenario.entryType ?? 'divination' } })
48:      };
49:    }
50:    if (u.includes('api.airtable.com') && opts.method === 'PATCH') {
51:      if (scenario.airtablePatchNetworkDown) throw new Error('network down');

279:      }
280:
281:      let verifiedUserId;
282:      try {
283:        ({ userId: verifiedUserId } = await resolveUserId(accessToken, LINE_CHANNEL_ID));
284:      } catch (e) {
285:        console.log("Deepdive access token validation failed", e.message || e);
286:        return json({ error: "Invalid access token" }, 401);
287:      }
288:
289:      const payloadIsObject = payload && typeof payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload);
290:      const payloadKeys = payloadIsObject ? Object.keys(payload) : [];
575:}
576:

578:  return {
579:    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin":  ALLOWED_ORIGIN,
580:    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET, POST, OPTIONS",
581:    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, X-Line-AccessToken",
582:  };
583:}

387:function openStampModal(logId, token) {
388:  stampState = { logId, token };
389:  const overlay = document.getElementById("stampOverlay");
390:  const textarea = document.getElementById("stampTextarea");
391:  const confirmBtn = document.getElementById("stampConfirmBtn");
392:  textarea.value = "";
393:  confirmBtn.disabled = false;
394:  overlay.classList.add("open");
395:  textarea.focus();
396:}
397:
398:function closeStampModal() {
399:  document.getElementById("stampOverlay").classList.remove("open");
400:}
401:
402:async function submitStamp() {
403:  const textarea = document.getElementById("stampTextarea");
404:  const confirmBtn = document.getElementById("stampConfirmBtn");
405:  const traceText = textarea.value.trim();
406:
407:  /* 無新增文案:空白/無 logId 靜默不送出(交由 textarea required 屬性攔),
408:     失敗分支僅 console.warn、保留入口按鈕,不彈出未定稿文案(見 plan §3 失敗分支) */
409:  if (!traceText || !stampState.logId) {
410:    return;
411:  }
412:
413:  confirmBtn.disabled = true;
414:
415:  try {
416:    const res = await fetch(`${RELAY_URL}trace`, {
417:      method: "POST",
418:      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-Line-AccessToken": stampState.token },
419:      body: JSON.stringify({ log_id: stampState.logId, trace_text: traceText }),
420:    });
421:    if (!res.ok) {
422:      console.warn("stamp submit failed", res.status);
423:      confirmBtn.disabled = false;
424:      return;
425:    }
426:    const data = await res.json();
427:    if (!data || typeof data.trace_text !== "string") {
428:      console.warn("stamp submit bad payload", data);
429:      confirmBtn.disabled = false;
430:      return;
431:    }
432:    closeStampModal();
433:    renderStampResult(data.trace_text);
434:  } catch (e) {
435:    console.warn("stamp submit error", e.message || e);
436:    confirmBtn.disabled = false;
437:  }
438:}
439:
440:function renderStampResult(traceText) {
441:  const openBtn = document.getElementById("stampOpenBtn");
442:  if (!openBtn) return;
443:  const section = openBtn.closest(".section");
444:  section.innerHTML = `
445:    <div class="stamp-toast">已蓋印。這一卦,有了後續。</div>
446:    ${buildStampBadgeHtml(traceText)}`;
447:}
448:
449:function wireStampHandlers(logId, token) {
450:  const openBtn = document.getElementById("stampOpenBtn");
451:  if (openBtn) {
452:    openBtn.addEventListener("click", () => openStampModal(logId, token));
453:  }
454:  document.getElementById("stampClose").addEventListener("click", closeStampModal);
455:  document.getElementById("stampConfirmBtn").addEventListener("click", submitStamp);
456:}

26:
27:## 1. 資料契約(新端點,不改既有端點)
28:
29:`POST /trace`
30:
31:請求:
32:```
33:Headers: X-Line-AccessToken: <token>, Content-Type: application/json
34:Body: { "log_id": "recXXXXXXXXXXXXXX", "trace_text": "<TA 輸入,1-500 字>" }
35:```
36:
37:行為(仿 `/log/seal` 既有模式,S37 紀律):
38:1. `OPTIONS` → 204 + `corsHeaders()`(既有全域 OPTIONS 分支已覆蓋,免另寫)。
39:2. Content-Type 非 json → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
40:3. body parse 失敗 → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
41:4. 缺 `X-Line-AccessToken` → 401 `Missing access token`。
42:5. `resolveUserId` 失敗(token 過期/非本 channel)→ 401 `Invalid access token`。
43:6. payload 白名單:僅允許 `log_id`、`trace_text` 兩鍵,多餘鍵 → 400 `Unexpected field in payload`。
44:7. `log_id` 非 `/^rec[a-zA-Z0-9]{14}$/` → 400 `Invalid log_id`。
45:8. `trace_text`:非字串 / trim 後空字串 / trim 後長度 >500 → 400 `Invalid trace_text`。
46:9. `AIRTABLE_API_KEY` 未配置 → 503(既有模式)。
47:10. GET 該筆 Divination_Log record:
48:    - fetch 失敗(network)→ 502 `Airtable read failed`。
49:    - 404 → 404 `{record:null}`。
50:    - 非 ok 非 404 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
51:    - JSON parse 失敗 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
52:11. **擁有權驗證**:`f.line_user_id_raw !== verifiedUserId` → **403** `Forbidden`(他人 log_id 寫入的機讀證據項,card 驗收 #2/#5)。
53:12. PATCH 該筆 `trace_text`(trim 後)+ `trace_at`(伺服端 `new Date().toISOString()`,+08:00 由 Airtable Date 欄時區顯示設定負責,伺服端一律存 UTC ISO,與 `golden_seal_time` 既有慣例一致)。
54:    - PATCH 失敗(network/非 ok)→ 502 `Airtable write failed`。
55:13. 成功 → 200 `{ traced: true, trace_text: "<trim後>", trace_at: "<ISO>" }`。
56:
57:**覆寫策略(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已修正措辭)**:明確定義為 **last-write-wins**,非「冪等」——重送同一請求會把 `trace_at` 更新為最新一次寫入的時間戳,結果本就會變,這是刻意設計而非缺陷。存取控制仍由 §步驟11 擁有權驗證(僅本人 LINE token 可寫自己的卦記)保證,前端隱藏入口按鈕只是體感引導,不是安全邊界,不作此宣稱。原「客服代補情境」提案已撤回(超出本卡範圍,若未來要開管理端點另立派工卡)。
58:
59:**記錄類型守門(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已採納)**:PATCH 前需再驗證 `(f.entry_type || "divination") !== "divination"` → 404(視為找不到,不寫入非卦記類型如 mood/deepdive/fupan)。
60:
61:**Request body 位元組上限(第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 已採納)**:改用 `request.text()` 讀 raw body,以 `TextEncoder` 量測位元組數,超過 `TRACE_MAX_BODY_BYTES = 4096` → **413** `Payload too large`,再對 raw text 做 `JSON.parse`(不再直接呼叫 `request.json()`,避免在做 500 字檢查前就無上限吃進整個 body)。
62:
63:**Content-Type 嚴格化(第 1 輪互審 SUGGEST 已採納)**:改用 `/^application\/json(?:;.*)?$/i` 比對整個 header 值,不再用寬鬆 `includes`,避免 `application/jsonp` 之類誤判通過。
64:
65:`/history` 與 `/log` 回傳欄位白名單追加 `trace_text`、`trace_at`(仿現行 `golden_seal`/`golden_seal_time` 追加方式,`airtableFetch` 的 `fields` 陣列與 `/log` 的 `record` 物件組裝處各加兩行)。
66:
67:**第 1 輪互審 BLOCKER 答辯(不採納,附理由)**:
68:- 「`resolveUserId` 把 LINE 429/5xx/逾時全部歸類成 401」——屬實,但 `resolveUserId` 是 `/log/seal`、`/trigger/deepdive`、`/trigger/fupan`、`POST /` 四個既有端點共用的函式,此問題在這些端點上早已存在,並非本次新增。§2 明確禁止修改 `resolveUserId`(diff 邊界鐵律,card 也未授權動共用函式)。此為既有系統性缺口,建議另立獨立派工卡統一修,不阻擋本卡(本卡新端點的錯誤分類與既有三個端點完全一致,不製造新的不一致)。`/trace` 的 catch 區塊已改為透傳 `e.status`/`e.message`(不再無條件寫死 401),為未來若修 `resolveUserId` 預留正確傳遞路徑。
69:

148:## 1. 資料契約(新端點,不改既有端點)
149:
150:`POST /trace`
151:
152:請求:
153:```
154:Headers: X-Line-AccessToken: <token>, Content-Type: application/json
155:Body: { "log_id": "recXXXXXXXXXXXXXX", "trace_text": "<TA 輸入,1-500 字>" }
156:```
157:
158:行為(仿 `/log/seal` 既有模式,S37 紀律):
159:1. `OPTIONS` → 204 + `corsHeaders()`(既有全域 OPTIONS 分支已覆蓋,免另寫)。
160:2. Content-Type 非 json → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
161:3. body parse 失敗 → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
162:4. 缺 `X-Line-AccessToken` → 401 `Missing access token`。
163:5. `resolveUserId` 失敗(token 過期/非本 channel)→ 401 `Invalid access token`。
164:6. payload 白名單:僅允許 `log_id`、`trace_text` 兩鍵,多餘鍵 → 400 `Unexpected field in payload`。
165:7. `log_id` 非 `/^rec[a-zA-Z0-9]{14}$/` → 400 `Invalid log_id`。
166:8. `trace_text`:非字串 / trim 後空字串 / trim 後長度 >500 → 400 `Invalid trace_text`。
167:9. `AIRTABLE_API_KEY` 未配置 → 503(既有模式)。
168:10. GET 該筆 Divination_Log record:
169:    - fetch 失敗(network)→ 502 `Airtable read failed`。
170:    - 404 → 404 `{record:null}`。
171:    - 非 ok 非 404 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
172:    - JSON parse 失敗 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
173:11. **擁有權驗證**:`f.line_user_id_raw !== verifiedUserId` → **403** `Forbidden`(他人 log_id 寫入的機讀證據項,card 驗收 #2/#5)。
174:12. PATCH 該筆 `trace_text`(trim 後)+ `trace_at`(伺服端 `new Date().toISOString()`,+08:00 由 Airtable Date 欄時區顯示設定負責,伺服端一律存 UTC ISO,與 `golden_seal_time` 既有慣例一致)。
175:    - PATCH 失敗(network/非 ok)→ 502 `Airtable write failed`。
176:13. 成功 → 200 `{ traced: true, trace_text: "<trim後>", trace_at: "<ISO>" }`。
177:
178:**冪等/覆寫**:允許重複蓋印覆寫既有 `trace_text`(card 文案「補後續」為單次體感,但無硬性鎖單多次的驗收要求;不新增 already_traced 分支,降低與 golden_seal 邏輯的混淆風險)。**第 1 輪互審後留意**:若 Codex 提出覆寫會讓 UI「一次性完成語」與可重複寫入矛盾,對案:前端當已有 `trace_text` 時不再顯示入口按鈕(見 §3),伺服端仍保留可覆寫的彈性給未來客服代補情境,兩者不衝突,無需鎖。
179:
180:`/history` 與 `/log` 回傳欄位白名單追加 `trace_text`、`trace_at`(仿現行 `golden_seal`/`golden_seal_time` 追加方式,`airtableFetch` 的 `fields` 陣列與 `/log` 的 `record` 物件組裝處各加兩行)。

266:## Reference Documentation
267:
268:- `references/rules.md` — all best practice rules with code examples and anti-patterns
269:- `references/review.md` — type validation, config validation, binding access patterns, review process
270:
271:## Rules Quick Reference
272:
273:### Configuration
274:
275:| Rule | Summary |
276:|------|---------|
277:| Compatibility date | Set `compatibility_date` to today on new projects; update periodically on existing ones |
278:| nodejs_compat | Enable the `nodejs_compat` flag — many libraries depend on Node.js built-ins |
279:| wrangler types | Run `wrangler types` to generate `Env` — never hand-write binding interfaces |
280:| Secrets | Use `wrangler secret put`, never hardcode secrets in config or source |
281:| wrangler.jsonc | Use JSONC config for non-secret settings — newer features are JSON-only |
282:
283:### Request & Response Handling
284:
285:| Rule | Summary |
286:|------|---------|
287:| Streaming | Stream large/unknown payloads — never `await response.text()` on unbounded data |
288:| waitUntil | Use `ctx.waitUntil()` for post-response work; do not destructure `ctx` |
289:
290:### Architecture
291:
292:| Rule | Summary |
293:|------|---------|
294:| Bindings over REST | Use in-process bindings (KV, R2, D1, Queues) — not the Cloudflare REST API |
295:| Queues & Workflows | Move async/background work off the critical path |
296:| Service bindings | Use service bindings for Worker-to-Worker calls — not public HTTP |
297:| Hyperdrive | Always use Hyperdrive for external PostgreSQL/MySQL connections |
298:
299:### Observability
300:
301:| Rule | Summary |
302:|------|---------|
303:| Logs & Traces | Enable `observability` in config with `head_sampling_rate`; use structured JSON logging |
304:
305:### Code Patterns
306:
307:| Rule | Summary |
308:|------|---------|
309:| No global request state | Never store request-scoped data in module-level variables |
310:| Floating promises | Every Promise must be `await`ed, `return`ed, `void`ed, or passed to `ctx.waitUntil()` |
311:
312:### Security
313:
314:| Rule | Summary |
315:|------|---------|
316:| Web Crypto | Use `crypto.randomUUID()` / `crypto.getRandomValues()` — never `Math.random()` for security |
317:| No passThroughOnException | Use explicit try/catch with structured error responses |
318:
319:## Anti-Patterns to Flag
320:
321:| Anti-pattern | Why it matters |
322:|-------------|----------------|
323:| `await response.text()` on unbounded data | Memory exhaustion — 128 MB limit |
324:| Hardcoded secrets in source or config | Credential leak via version control |
325:| `Math.random()` for tokens/IDs | Predictable, not cryptographically secure |
326:| Bare `fetch()` without `await` or `waitUntil` | Floating promise — dropped result, swallowed error |
327:| Module-level mutable variables for request state | Cross-request data leaks, stale state, I/O errors |
328:| Cloudflare REST API from inside a Worker | Unnecessary network hop, auth overhead, added latency |
329:| `ctx.passThroughOnException()` as error handling | Hides bugs, makes debugging impossible |
330:| Hand-written `Env` interface | Drifts from actual wrangler config bindings |
331:| Direct string comparison for secret values | Timing side-channel — use `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual` |
332:| Destructuring `ctx` (`const { waitUntil } = ctx`) | Loses `this` binding — throws "Illegal invocation" at runtime |
333:| `any` on `Env` or handler params | Defeats type safety for all binding access |
334:| `as unknown as T` double-cast | Hides real type incompatibilities — fix the design |
335:| `implements` on platform base classes (instead of `extends`) | Legacy — loses `this.ctx`, `this.env`. Applies to DurableObject, WorkerEntrypoint, Workflow |
336:| `env.X` inside platform base class | Should be `this.env.X` in classes extending DurableObject, WorkerEntrypoint, etc. |
337:
338:## Review Workflow
339:
340:1. **Retrieve** — fetch latest best practices page, workers types, and wrangler schema
341:2. **Read full files** — not just diffs; context matters for binding access patterns
342:3. **Check types** — binding access, handler signatures, no `any`, no unsafe casts (see `references/review.md`)
343:4. **Check config** — compatibility_date, nodejs_compat, observability, secrets, binding-code consistency
344:5. **Check patterns** — streaming, floating promises, global state, serialization boundaries
345:6. **Check security** — crypto usage, secret handling, timing-safe comparisons, error handling
346:7. **Validate with tools** — `npx tsc --noEmit`, lint for `no-floating-promises`
347:8. **Reference rules** — see `references/rules.md` for each rule's correct pattern
348:
349:## Scope
350:
351:This skill covers Workers-specific best practices and code review. For related topics:
352:
353:- **Durable Objects**: load the `durable-objects` skill
354:- **Workflows**: see [Rules of Workflows](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workflows/build/rules-of-workflows/)
355:- **Wrangler CLI commands**: load the `wrangler` skill
356:
357:## Principles
358:
359:- **Be certain.** Retrieve before flagging. If unsure about an API, config field, or pattern, fetch the docs first.
360:- **Provide evidence.** Reference line numbers, tool output, or docs links.
361:- **Focus on what developers will copy.** Workers code in examples and docs gets pasted into production.
362:- **Correctness over completeness.** A concise example that works beats a comprehensive one with errors.

837:# Workers Best Practices — Rules
838:
839:Each rule has an imperative summary, what to check, the correct pattern, and an anti-pattern where applicable. Code examples are plain TypeScript — no MDX components.
840:
841:When a rule involves config fields or API signatures that may evolve, a **Retrieve** callout reminds you to check the latest docs or types before flagging. All doc paths are relative to `https://developers.cloudflare.com`.

843:---
844:
845:## Configuration
846:
847:### Keep compatibility_date current
848:
849:Set `compatibility_date` to today on new projects. Update periodically on existing ones to access new APIs and fixes.
850:
851:**Check**: `compatibility_date` exists. Flag if older than 6 months.
852:
853:```jsonc
854:// wrangler.jsonc
855:{
856:  "compatibility_date": "$today",  // Replace with today's date (YYYY-MM-DD)
857:  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"]
858:}
859:```
860:
861:**Retrieve**: current compatibility dates at `/workers/configuration/compatibility-dates/`.
862:
863:### Enable nodejs_compat
864:
865:The `nodejs_compat` flag enables Node.js built-in modules (`node:crypto`, `node:buffer`, `node:stream`). Many libraries require it. Missing this flag causes cryptic import errors at runtime.
866:
867:**Check**: `compatibility_flags` includes `"nodejs_compat"`.
868:
869:```jsonc
870:{
871:  "compatibility_flags": ["nodejs_compat"]
872:}
873:```
874:
875:### Generate binding types with wrangler types
876:
877:Never hand-write the `Env` interface. Run `wrangler types` to generate it from the wrangler config. Re-run after adding or renaming any binding.
878:
879:**Check**: no manually defined `Env` or `interface Env` that duplicates wrangler config bindings. Look for `satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>` pattern on the default export.
880:
881:```ts
882:// Generated by wrangler types — always matches actual config
883:export default {
884:  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
885:    const value = await env.MY_KV.get("key");
886:    return new Response(value);
887:  },
888:} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
889:```
890:
891:Anti-pattern:
892:```ts
893:// Hand-written Env that drifts from actual bindings
894:interface Env {
895:  MY_KV: KVNamespace;  // What if the binding name changed?
896:}
897:```
898:
899:### Store secrets with wrangler secret
900:
901:Secrets must never appear in wrangler config or source code. Use `wrangler secret put` and access via `env` at runtime. Non-secret config goes in `vars`.
902:
903:**Check**: no string literals that look like API keys, tokens, or credentials. Verify `.env` is in `.gitignore` for local dev.
904:
905:```jsonc
906:{
907:  "vars": {
908:    "API_BASE_URL": "https://api.example.com"  // Non-secret: OK in config
909:  }
910:  // Secrets set via: wrangler secret put API_KEY
911:}
912:```
913:
914:Anti-pattern:
915:```jsonc
916:{
917:  "vars": {
918:    "API_KEY": "sk-live-abc123..."  // Secret in version control
919:  }
920:}
921:```
922:
923:### Use wrangler.jsonc for config
924:
925:Prefer `wrangler.jsonc` over `wrangler.toml`. Newer features are JSON-only. JSONC supports comments for documenting config decisions.
926:
927:**Check**: project uses `wrangler.jsonc` (or `wrangler.json`). Flag `wrangler.toml` in new projects.
928:
929:---
930:
931:## Request & Response Handling
932:
933:### Stream request and response bodies
934:
935:Workers have a 128 MB memory limit. Buffering entire bodies with `await response.text()` or `await request.arrayBuffer()` crashes on large payloads. Stream data through using `TransformStream` or pass `response.body` directly.
936:
937:**Check**: any `await response.text()`, `await response.json()`, or `await response.arrayBuffer()` on data that could be large or unbounded. Small, bounded payloads (known-size JSON, config files) are fine to buffer.
938:
939:Correct — stream through:
940:```ts
941:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
942:  const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/large-dataset");
943:  return new Response(response.body, response);
944:}
945:```
946:
947:Correct — concatenate multiple streams:
948:```ts
949:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
950:  const urls = ["https://api.example.com/part-1", "https://api.example.com/part-2"];
951:  const { readable, writable } = new TransformStream();
952:
953:  // Track the pipeline promise — don't let it float
954:  ctx.waitUntil((async () => {
955:    for (const url of urls) {
956:      const response = await fetch(url);
957:      if (response.body) {
958:        await response.body.pipeTo(writable, { preventClose: true });
959:      }
960:    }
961:    await writable.close();
962:  })());
963:
964:  return new Response(readable, {
965:    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream" },
966:  });
967:}
968:```
969:
970:Anti-pattern:
971:```ts
972:// Buffers entire body — crashes on large payloads
973:const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/large-dataset");
974:const text = await response.text();
975:return new Response(text);
976:```
977:
978:**Retrieve**: streaming APIs at `/workers/runtime-apis/streams/`.
979:
980:### Use waitUntil for work after the response
981:
982:`ctx.waitUntil()` performs background work (analytics, cache writes, webhooks) after the response is sent. Keeps response fast. 30-second time limit after response.
983:
984:**Check**: background work uses `ctx.waitUntil()`, not inline `await`. Do not destructure `ctx` — it loses the `this` binding and throws "Illegal invocation".
985:
986:```ts
987:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
988:  const data = await processRequest(request);
989:
990:  ctx.waitUntil(logToAnalytics(env, data));
991:  ctx.waitUntil(updateCache(env, data));
992:
993:  return Response.json(data);
994:}
995:```
996:
997:Anti-pattern:
998:```ts
999:// Destructuring ctx loses the this binding
1000:const { waitUntil } = ctx;  // "Illegal invocation" at runtime
1001:waitUntil(somePromise);
1002:```
1003:
1004:---
1005:
1006:## Architecture
1007:
1008:### Use bindings for Cloudflare services, not REST APIs
1009:
1010:Bindings (KV, R2, D1, Queues, Workflows) are direct, in-process references — no network hop, no authentication, no extra latency. Using the Cloudflare REST API from a Worker wastes time and adds complexity.
1011:
1012:**Check**: no `fetch("https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/...")` calls for services available as bindings.
1013:
1014:```ts
1015:// Binding — direct, zero-cost
1016:const object = await env.MY_BUCKET.get("my-file");
1017:```
1018:
1019:Anti-pattern:
1020:```ts
1021:// REST API from inside a Worker — unnecessary overhead
1022:const response = await fetch(
1023:  "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/.../r2/buckets/.../objects/my-file",
1024:  { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${env.CF_API_TOKEN}` } }
1025:);
1026:```
1027:
1028:### Use Queues and Workflows for async and background work
1029:
1030:Long-running, retriable, or non-urgent tasks should not block a request.
1031:
1032:- **Queues**: decouple producer from consumer. Fan-out, buffering/batching, simple single-step background jobs. At-least-once delivery.
1033:- **Workflows**: multi-step durable execution. Each step's return value is persisted; only failed steps retry. Can run for hours/days/weeks.
1034:- **Both together**: Queue buffers high-throughput entry, consumer creates Workflow instances for complex processing.
1035:
1036:**Check**: long-running work (email sends, webhooks, multi-step processes) is offloaded to Queues or Workflows, not done inline in the fetch handler.
1037:
1038:```ts
1039:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
1040:  const order = await request.json<{ id: string; type: string }>();
1041:
1042:  if (order.type === "simple") {
1043:    await env.ORDER_QUEUE.send({ orderId: order.id, action: "send-email" });
1044:  } else {
1045:    await env.FULFILLMENT_WORKFLOW.create({ params: { orderId: order.id } });
1046:  }
1047:
1048:  return Response.json({ status: "accepted" }, { status: 202 });
1049:}
1050:```
1051:
1052:**Retrieve**: `/queues/` and `/workflows/` for current APIs. For Workflow-specific rules, see [Rules of Workflows](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workflows/build/rules-of-workflows/).
1053:
1054:### Use service bindings for Worker-to-Worker communication
1055:
1056:Service bindings are zero-cost, bypass the public internet, and support type-safe RPC. Do not call another Worker via its public URL.
1057:
1058:**Check**: Worker-to-Worker calls use `env.SERVICE_NAME.method()` (RPC) or `env.SERVICE_NAME.fetch()`, not `fetch("https://my-other-worker.example.com/...")`.
1059:
1060:```ts
1061:import { WorkerEntrypoint } from "cloudflare:workers";
1062:
1063:export class AuthService extends WorkerEntrypoint {
1064:  async verifyToken(token: string): Promise<{ userId: string; valid: boolean }> {
1065:    return { userId: "user-123", valid: true };
1066:  }
1067:}
1068:
1069:// Caller Worker
1070:const auth = await env.AUTH_SERVICE.verifyToken(token);
1071:```
1072:
1073:**Retrieve**: verify `WorkerEntrypoint` import path and signature against latest `@cloudflare/workers-types`.
1074:
1075:### Use Hyperdrive for external database connections
1076:
1077:Hyperdrive maintains a regional connection pool, eliminating per-request TCP + TLS + auth cost (often 300-500ms). Create a new `Client` per request — Hyperdrive manages the underlying pool. Requires `nodejs_compat`.
1078:
1079:**Check**: any `new Client()` or database connection that uses a direct connection string instead of `env.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString`.
1080:
1081:```jsonc
1082:{
1083:  "hyperdrive": [{ "binding": "HYPERDRIVE", "id": "<YOUR_HYPERDRIVE_ID>" }]
1084:}
1085:```
1086:
1087:```ts
1088:import { Client } from "pg";
1089:
1090:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
1091:  const client = new Client({ connectionString: env.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString });
1092:  await client.connect();
1093:  const result = await client.query("SELECT id, name FROM users LIMIT 10");
1094:  return Response.json(result.rows);
1095:}
1096:```
1097:
1098:**Retrieve**: `/hyperdrive/` for current configuration and supported databases.
1099:
1100:---
1101:
1102:## Observability
1103:
1104:### Enable Workers Logs and Traces
1105:
1106:Enable `observability` in wrangler config before deploying to production. Use `head_sampling_rate` to control volume and cost. Use structured JSON logging — `console.log(JSON.stringify({...}))` — so logs are searchable. Use `console.error` for errors (appears at error severity in the dashboard).
1107:
1108:**Check**: `observability.enabled` is `true` in config. Logging uses structured JSON, not string concatenation.
1109:
1110:```jsonc
1111:{
1112:  "observability": {
1113:    "enabled": true,
1114:    "logs": { "head_sampling_rate": 1 },
1115:    "traces": { "enabled": true, "head_sampling_rate": 0.01 }
1116:  }
1117:}
1118:```
1119:
1120:```ts
1121:// Structured JSON — searchable and filterable
1122:console.log(JSON.stringify({ message: "incoming request", method: request.method, path: url.pathname }));
1123:
1124:// Error severity
1125:console.error(JSON.stringify({ message: "request failed", error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) }));
1126:```
1127:
1128:Anti-pattern:
1129:```ts
1130:// Unstructured string logs — hard to query
1131:console.log("Got a request to " + url.pathname);
1132:```
1133:
1134:**Retrieve**: `/workers/observability/logs/workers-logs/` and `/workers/observability/traces/` for current config options.
1135:
1136:---
1137:
1138:## Code Patterns
1139:
1140:### Do not store request-scoped state in global scope
1141:
1142:Workers reuse isolates across requests. Module-level mutable variables cause cross-request data leaks, stale state, and "Cannot perform I/O on behalf of a different request" errors.
1143:
1144:**Check**: no mutable `let`/`var` at module scope that gets assigned inside a handler. Pass state through function arguments.
1145:
1146:```ts
1147:export default {
1148:  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
1149:    const userId = request.headers.get("X-User-Id");
1150:    const result = await handleRequest(userId, env);
1151:    return Response.json(result);
1152:  },
1153:} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
1154:```
1155:
1156:Anti-pattern:
1157:```ts
1158:// Module-level mutable state — leaks between requests
1159:let currentUser: string | null = null;
1160:
1161:export default {
1162:  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
1163:    currentUser = request.headers.get("X-User-Id");  // Visible to next request
1164:    // ...
1165:  },
1166:};
1167:```
1168:
1169:### Always await or waitUntil Promises
1170:
1171:A Promise that is not `await`ed, `return`ed, or passed to `ctx.waitUntil()` is a floating promise. Causes: dropped results, swallowed errors, unfinished work. The runtime may terminate the isolate before it completes.
1172:
1173:**Check**: every `fetch()`, `env.*.put()`, `env.*.send()`, and any other async call is handled. Enable `no-floating-promises` lint rule.
1174:
1175:```bash
1176:# ESLint
1177:npx eslint --rule '{"@typescript-eslint/no-floating-promises": "error"}' src/
1178:
1179:# oxlint
1180:npx oxlint --deny typescript/no-floating-promises src/
1181:```
1182:
1183:```ts
1184:// Correct: await when you need the result
1185:const response = await fetch("https://api.example.com/process", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) });
1186:
1187:// Correct: waitUntil when you don't need the result before responding
1188:ctx.waitUntil(fetch("https://api.example.com/webhook", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) }));
1189:```
1190:
1191:Anti-pattern:
1192:```ts
1193:// Floating promise — result dropped, error swallowed
1194:fetch("https://api.example.com/webhook", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(data) });
1195:```
1196:
1197:### Be aware of platform limits
1198:
1199:Workers have a 10ms CPU time limit (Bundled) or 30s (Standard/Unbound). Heavy synchronous work — tight loops, large JSON parsing, compute-intensive crypto — can hit the CPU limit and terminate the request.
1200:
1201:**Check**: compute-heavy operations that run synchronously. Consider breaking work into smaller chunks, offloading to Queues/Workflows, or using WebAssembly for CPU-intensive tasks.
1202:
1203:**Retrieve**: current limits at `/workers/platform/limits/`.
1204:
1205:---
1206:
1207:## Security
1208:
1209:### Use Web Crypto for secure token generation
1210:
1211:Use `crypto.randomUUID()` for unique IDs and `crypto.getRandomValues()` for random bytes. `Math.random()` is not cryptographically secure.
1212:
1213:For comparing secrets (API keys, HMAC signatures), use `crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual()`. Hash both values to a fixed size first — do not short-circuit on length mismatch (leaks length via timing).
1214:
1215:**Check**: no `Math.random()` for security-sensitive values. Secret comparisons use `timingSafeEqual` with fixed-size hashing.
1216:
1217:```ts
1218:// Secure random UUID
1219:const sessionId = crypto.randomUUID();
1220:
1221:// Secure random bytes
1222:const tokenBytes = new Uint8Array(32);
1223:crypto.getRandomValues(tokenBytes);
1224:const token = Array.from(tokenBytes).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
1225:```
1226:
1227:```ts
1228:// Constant-time comparison — hash first to avoid length leak
1229:async function verifyToken(provided: string, expected: string): Promise<boolean> {
1230:  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
1231:  const [providedHash, expectedHash] = await Promise.all([
1232:    crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoder.encode(provided)),
1233:    crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", encoder.encode(expected)),
1234:  ]);
1235:  return crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual(providedHash, expectedHash);
1236:}
1237:```
1238:
1239:Anti-pattern:
1240:```ts
1241:// Predictable — not cryptographically secure
1242:const token = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2);
1243:
1244:// Timing side-channel — leaks information about the expected value
1245:return provided === expected;
1246:```
1247:
1248:**Retrieve**: `/workers/runtime-apis/web-crypto/` for current API surface.
1249:
1250:### Explicit error handling over passThroughOnException
1251:
1252:`passThroughOnException()` is a fail-open mechanism that sends requests to the origin when the Worker throws. It hides bugs and makes debugging difficult. Use explicit try/catch with structured error responses.
1253:
1254:**Check**: no `ctx.passThroughOnException()` calls. Error handling uses try/catch with structured JSON error responses and `console.error`.
1255:
1256:```ts
1257:async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<Response> {
1258:  try {
1259:    const result = await handleRequest(request, env);
1260:    return Response.json(result);
1261:  } catch (error) {
1262:    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error";
1263:    console.error(JSON.stringify({ message: "unhandled error", error: message, path: new URL(request.url).pathname }));
1264:    return Response.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, { status: 500 });
1265:  }
1266:}
1267:```
1268:
1269:---
1270:
1271:## Development & Testing
1272:
1273:### Test with @cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers
1274:
1275:Runs tests inside the Workers runtime with real bindings. Catches issues that Node.js-based tests miss.
1276:
1277:**Known pitfall**: the Vitest pool auto-injects `nodejs_compat`, so tests pass even if your wrangler config is missing the flag. Always confirm your `wrangler.jsonc` includes `nodejs_compat` if your code depends on Node.js built-ins.
1278:
1279:**Check**: test setup uses `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers`. Tests cover nullable returns (e.g., KV `.get()` returning `null`).
1280:
1281:```ts
1282:import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
1283:import { env } from "cloudflare:test";
1284:
1285:describe("KV operations", () => {
1286:  it("should store and retrieve a value", async () => {
1287:    await env.MY_KV.put("key", "value");
1288:    const result = await env.MY_KV.get("key");
1289:    expect(result).toBe("value");
1290:  });
1291:
1292:  it("should return null for missing keys", async () => {
1293:    const result = await env.MY_KV.get("nonexistent");
1294:    expect(result).toBeNull();
1295:  });
1296:});
1297:```
1298:
1299:**Retrieve**: `/workers/testing/vitest-integration/` for current setup and configuration.
1300:FILE: C:\Users\ivych\.codex\skills\workers-best-practices\references\review.md
1301:# Code Review — Workers
1302:
1303:How to review Workers code for type correctness, API usage, config validity, and best practices. This is self-contained — do not assume access to other skills.
1304:
1305:## Retrieval
1306:
1307:Prefer retrieval over pre-training. Types, config schemas, and APIs change with compatibility dates and new bindings.
1308:
1309:### Workers types
1310:
1311:Fetch the latest `@cloudflare/workers-types` before reviewing. The project may have an older version installed.
1312:
1313:```bash
1314:mkdir -p /tmp/workers-types-latest && \
1315:  npm pack @cloudflare/workers-types --pack-destination /tmp/workers-types-latest && \
1316:  tar -xzf /tmp/workers-types-latest/cloudflare-workers-types-*.tgz -C /tmp/workers-types-latest
1317:# Types are at /tmp/workers-types-latest/package/index.d.ts
1318:```
1319:
1320:Search this file for the specific type, class, or interface under review. Do not guess type names.
1321:
1322:Alternative: `npx wrangler types` generates a typed `Env` interface from the local wrangler config.
1323:
1324:Fallback: read `node_modules/@cloudflare/workers-types/index.d.ts`. Note the installed version.
1325:
1326:### Wrangler config schema
1327:
1328:The authoritative schema is bundled with wrangler as `config-schema.json` (JSON Schema draft-07).
1329:
1330:```bash
1331:# Read from local node_modules
1332:cat node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json
1333:```
1334:
1335:Do not guess field names or structures — look them up.
1336:
1337:### Cloudflare docs
1338:
1339:Use the Cloudflare docs search tool if available, or fetch from `https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/`. The best practices page lives at `/workers/best-practices/workers-best-practices/`.
1340:
1341:---
1342:
1343:## Type Validation
1344:
1345:### Env interface
1346:
1347:- Every binding must have a specific type. Flag `any`, `unknown`, `object`, or `Record<string, unknown>` on bindings.
1348:- Binding types that accept generic parameters (Durable Object namespaces, Queues, Service bindings for RPC) must include them. Read the type definition to confirm which types are generic.
1349:- Binding names must match the wrangler config exactly.
1350:- Prefer generated types from `wrangler types` over hand-written interfaces.
1351:
1352:### Handler and class signatures
1353:
1354:Verify against current type definitions — do not assume signatures are stable.
1355:
1356:- Correct import path (most Workers platform classes import from `"cloudflare:workers"`)
1357:- Generic type parameter on base classes (e.g., `DurableObject<Env>`)
1358:- Binding access pattern: `env.X` in module export handlers, `this.env.X` in classes extending platform base classes
1359:- `ExecutionContext` as the third param in module export handlers (needed for `ctx.waitUntil()`)
1360:- `fetch()` handlers must return `Promise<Response>`
1361:
1362:### Binding access — the most common error
1363:
1364:- **Module export handlers** (`fetch`, `scheduled`, `queue`, `email`): bindings via `env.X` parameter
1365:- **Platform base classes** (`WorkerEntrypoint`, `DurableObject`, `Workflow`, `Agent`): bindings via `this.env.X`
1366:
1367:Flag `env.X` inside a class extending a platform base class. Flag `this.env.X` inside a module export handler.
1368:
1369:### Type integrity rules
1370:
1371:| Rule | Detail |
1372:|------|--------|
1373:| No `any` | Never on binding types, handler params, or API responses |
1374:| No double-casting | `as unknown as T` hides real incompatibilities — fix the underlying design |
1375:| Justify suppressions | `@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error` must include a comment explaining why |
1376:| Prefer `satisfies` | Use `satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>` over `as` — validates without widening |
1377:| Validate, do not assert | Schema or type guard for untyped data (JSON, parsed bodies), not `as` |
1378:
1379:### Stale class patterns
1380:
1381:Old patterns survive in codebases long after APIs change.
1382:
1383:- **`extends` vs `implements`**: platform classes use `extends`, not `implements`. The `implements` pattern is legacy and loses `this.ctx`, `this.env`.
1384:- **Import paths**: verify module specifiers match what types actually export. Common mistake: wrong path for `"cloudflare:workers"` vs `"cloudflare:workflows"`.
1385:- **Renamed properties**: e.g., `this.state` to `this.ctx` in Durable Objects. Search types to confirm.
1386:- **Constructor signatures**: base class constructors change. Verify expected parameters.
1387:
1388:---
1389:
1390:## Config Validation
1391:
1392:### Required fields
1393:
1394:For executable examples, verify: `name`, `compatibility_date`, `main`. Check the schema for current required fields.
1395:
1396:### Config format
1397:
1398:- **JSONC** (`wrangler.jsonc`) — preferred for new projects
1399:- **JSON** (`wrangler.json`) — valid but no comments
1400:- **TOML** (`wrangler.toml`) — legacy; acceptable in existing content, flag in new projects
1401:
1402:### Binding-code consistency
1403:
1404:1. Every `env.X` reference in code has a corresponding binding declaration in config
1405:2. Every binding in config is referenced in code (warn on unused)
1406:3. Names match exactly (case-sensitive)
1407:4. For Durable Objects: `class_name` matches the exported class name
1408:
1409:### Common config mistakes
1410:
1411:| Check | What to look for |
1412:|-------|-----------------|
1413:| Stale `compatibility_date` | Should be recent; use `$today` placeholder in docs |
1414:| Missing DO migrations | Every new DO class needs a migration entry |
1415:| Binding name mismatch | Config `binding`/`name` must match `env.X` in code |
1416:| Secrets in config | Never in `vars` — use `wrangler secret put` |
1417:| Wrong binding key | Verify top-level key name against the schema |
1418:| Missing entrypoint | `main` required for executable Workers |
1419:
1420:---
1421:
1422:## Anti-Patterns to Flag
1423:
1424:See the full anti-patterns table in `SKILL.md`. The type-specific ones to watch for during review:
1425:
1426:- **`any` on `Env` or handler params** — defeats type safety for all downstream binding access
1427:- **`as unknown as T`** — hides real type incompatibilities; fix the underlying design
1428:- **`@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error` without explanation** — masks errors silently; require a justifying comment
1429:- **`implements` instead of `extends` on platform base classes** — legacy pattern; loses `this.ctx`, `this.env`
1430:- **`env.X` inside class body** — should be `this.env.X` in platform base classes
1431:- **`this.env.X` in module export handler** — should be `env.X` parameter
1432:- **Non-serializable values across boundaries** — `Response`, `Error` in step/queue compiles but fails at runtime
1433:
1434:---
1435:
1436:## Serialization Boundaries
1437:
1438:Data crossing these boundaries must be structured-clone serializable:
1439:
1440:- **Queue messages**: body passed to `.send()` or `.sendBatch()`
1441:- **Workflow step return values**: persisted to durable storage
1442:- **DO storage**: values in `storage.put()` or SQL
1443:- **`postMessage()`**: WebSocket messages
1444:
1445:Non-serializable types to flag: `Response`, `Request`, `Error`, functions, class instances with methods, `Map`/`Set`, `Symbol`.
1446:
1447:Valid: plain objects, arrays, strings, numbers, booleans, null, `ArrayBuffer`, `Date`.
1448:
1449:---
1450:
1451:## Review Process
1452:
1453:1. **Retrieve** — fetch latest workers types, wrangler schema, and best practices page
1454:2. **Read full files** — not just diffs; context matters for binding access patterns
1455:3. **Categorize code** — determines what to check:
1456:   - **Illustrative** (concept demo, comments for most logic): verify correct API names and realistic signatures
1457:   - **Demonstrative** (functional snippet, would work in context): verify syntax, correct APIs, correct binding access
1458:   - **Executable** (standalone, runs without modification): verify compiles, runs, includes imports and config
1459:4. **Check types** — binding access pattern, handler signatures, no `any`, no unsafe casts
1460:5. **Check config** — compatibility_date, nodejs_compat, observability, secrets, binding-code consistency
1461:6. **Check patterns** — streaming, floating promises, global state, serialization boundaries
1462:7. **Check security** — crypto usage, secret handling, timing-safe comparisons, error handling
1463:8. **Validate with tools** — `npx tsc --noEmit`, lint for `no-floating-promises`
1464:9. **Assess risk** — HIGH (auth, crypto, bindings), MEDIUM (business logic, config), LOW (style, comments)
1465:
1466:### Output format
1467:
1468:```
1469:**[SEVERITY]** Brief description
1470:`file.ts:42` — explanation with evidence
1471:Suggested fix: `code`
1472:```
1473:
1474:Severity: **CRITICAL** (security, data loss, crash) | **HIGH** (type error, wrong API, broken config) | **MEDIUM** (missing validation, edge case) | **LOW** (style, minor improvement)

1712:## 1. 資料契約(新端點,不改既有端點)
1713:
1714:`POST /trace`
1715:
1716:請求:
1717:```
1718:Headers: X-Line-AccessToken: <token>, Content-Type: application/json
1719:Body: { "log_id": "recXXXXXXXXXXXXXX", "trace_text": "<TA 輸入,1-500 字>" }
1720:```
1721:
1722:行為(仿 `/log/seal` 既有模式,S37 紀律):
1723:1. `OPTIONS` → 204 + `corsHeaders()`(既有全域 OPTIONS 分支已覆蓋,免另寫)。
1724:2. Content-Type 非 json → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
1725:3. body parse 失敗 → 400 `Bad JSON body`。
1726:4. 缺 `X-Line-AccessToken` → 401 `Missing access token`。
1727:5. `resolveUserId` 失敗(token 過期/非本 channel)→ 401 `Invalid access token`。
1728:6. payload 白名單:僅允許 `log_id`、`trace_text` 兩鍵,多餘鍵 → 400 `Unexpected field in payload`。
1729:7. `log_id` 非 `/^rec[a-zA-Z0-9]{14}$/` → 400 `Invalid log_id`。
1730:8. `trace_text`:非字串 / trim 後空字串 / trim 後長度 >500 → 400 `Invalid trace_text`。
1731:9. `AIRTABLE_API_KEY` 未配置 → 503(既有模式)。
1732:10. GET 該筆 Divination_Log record:
1733:    - fetch 失敗(network)→ 502 `Airtable read failed`。
1734:    - 404 → 404 `{record:null}`。
1735:    - 非 ok 非 404 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
1736:    - JSON parse 失敗 → 502 `Airtable read failed`。
1737:11. **擁有權驗證**:`f.line_user_id_raw !== verifiedUserId` → **403** `Forbidden`(他人 log_id 寫入的機讀證據項,card 驗收 #2/#5)。
1738:12. PATCH 該筆 `trace_text`(trim 後)+ `trace_at`(伺服端 `new Date().toISOString()`,+08:00 由 Airtable Date 欄時區顯示設定負責,伺服端一律存 UTC ISO,與 `golden_seal_time` 既有慣例一致)。
1739:    - PATCH 失敗(network/非 ok)→ 502 `Airtable write failed`。
1740:13. 成功 → 200 `{ traced: true, trace_text: "<trim後>", trace_at: "<ISO>" }`。
1741:
1742:**冪等/覆寫**:允許重複蓋印覆寫既有 `trace_text`(card 文案「補後續」為單次體感,但無硬性鎖單多次的驗收要求;不新增 already_traced 分支,降低與 golden_seal 邏輯的混淆風險)。**第 1 輪互審後留意**:若 Codex 提出覆寫會讓 UI「一次性完成語」與可重複寫入矛盾,對案:前端當已有 `trace_text` 時不再顯示入口按鈕(見 §3),伺服端仍保留可覆寫的彈性給未來客服代補情境,兩者不衝突,無需鎖。
1743:
1744:`/history` 與 `/log` 回傳欄位白名單追加 `trace_text`、`trace_at`(仿現行 `golden_seal`/`golden_seal_time` 追加方式,`airtableFetch` 的 `fields` 陣列與 `/log` 的 `record` 物件組裝處各加兩行)。
1745:

1851:- 新增 1 個 route 分支 `POST /trace`(插入位置:緊接既有 `POST /log/seal` 分支之後,`POST /trigger/deepdive` 之前;不打散既有分支順序)。
1852:- `/history` fields 陣列追加 `"trace_text","trace_at"`;`records.map` 組裝物件追加 `trace_text: f.trace_text || null, trace_at: f.trace_at || null`。
1853:- `/log` 的 `record` 組裝物件同樣追加兩欄。
1854:- **不修改**:`POST /`、`/study`、`/log/seal`、`/trigger/deepdive`、`/trigger/fupan`、`resolveUserId`、`airtableFetch`、`readQuotaGate`、`corsHeaders`、`json`、`isValidIsoDateString`、任何常數。
1855:- 交付物:完整 `worker.js`(供逐行 diff),不含 wrangler.toml/secrets(未變動)。

1857:## 3. log.html 蓋印互動(文案逐字,零改字——card §二第2項唯一合法字面)
1858:
1859:- 入口按鈕(僅當該筆卦記 `trace_text` 為空/null 時顯示):`補後續 · 蓋金印`
1860:- 點擊 → 開啟既有 modal 慣例(仿 `mingge_guaji_main_prototype_v0_2.html` btn-stamp 流程,沿用現有 `.modal`/`.sheet` class 與墨綠金 tokens,不新造美學元件):
1861:  - textarea placeholder:`當時所問的事,後來往哪邊走了 —— 一兩句即可。`
1862:  - 確認按鈕:`蓋下金印`
1863:- 確認送出成功(僅本次顯示,不常駐):`已蓋印。這一卦,有了後續。`
1864:- 之後常駐(取代入口按鈕位置):`金印 · 已補後續` + 其下顯示 `trace_text` 內容(唯讀文字,非再編輯輸入框——card 未給編輯文案,不自創)。
1865:- 失敗分支(仿 goldenseal 前端既有慣例,非文案表列項,屬工程必要的 fallback):fetch reject / 非 200 / json 契約不合 → 保留入口按鈕、不寫入常駐態,不彈出未定稿文案(僅 console.warn,不打斷 TA)。
1866:- **禁動**:index.html 本卡不碰;log.html 既有 `.topbar .seal`(顯示「命」字圓形裝飾,與本次金印無關,S137 命名時已刻意避開衝突)不改動、不共用 class 名。

1868:## 4. 回歸鉤 tests/
1869:
1870:新增 `tests/test_e25_stamp_v1_0.sh`,仿現行 `tests/test_e40_giftfix_v1_0.sh` 風格(Node vm 抽函式 + headless fetch mock):

1874:  - case3: 缺 token → 401。

1879:  - 斷言 5 條文案字串逐字存在(§3 列表),且**只出現一次定義**(避免多處硬編碼分裂真相源)。

3118:
3119:- [BLOCKER] 未驗證記錄類型。只比對 `line_user_id_raw`，同一使用者在 `Divination_Log` 中的非 `divination` 記錄也能被寫入。PATCH 前須驗證 `entry_type === "divination"`。
3120:
3121:- [BLOCKER] Request body 無大小上限。`request.json()` 會在 500 字檢查前完整載入內容；巨大 JSON 或大量前置空白可造成記憶體／CPU 濫用。須先設 byte 上限並以 `413 Payload Too Large` 拒絕，不能只依 trim 後字數。
3122:
3123:- [BLOCKER] LINE 驗證錯誤被錯誤歸類。`resolveUserId` 同時涉及 verify、profile、網路與 JSON 解析；LINE 429/5xx、逾時、回傳壞 JSON 都不應被當成 `401 Invalid access token`。只有明確 token 無效／channel 不符回 401，其餘應回 502/503，並加入對應測試。
3124:
3125:- [BLOCKER] 所謂「端到端 PATCH 實測」沒有驗收路徑。§5 全部是 dry-run、mock 與 grep，沒有實際 Airtable PATCH、read-back 或回復策略。須將驗收拆成「無密鑰的 pre-deploy mock」與「Perth 授權後的 canary E2E」，並明定測試 record、讀回比對及清理方式；否則不得宣稱 E2E 完成。
3126:
3127:- [BLOCKER] Worker 真相源不完整。live bundle 不包含 `compatibility_date`、flags、vars、bindings、routes 等部署契約；只保存 `worker.js` 無法安全重建或 diff live Worker。`wrangler deploy --dry-run` 也未指定工作目錄或 config，可能使用錯誤設定或自動偵測。須先取得並凍結 live metadata，再用明確的 `--config`／`--cwd` 驗證。Cloudflare亦建議把 Wrangler configuration 當作部署設定真相源。[Wrangler 文件](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/wrangler/commands/workers/)
3128:
3129:- [BLOCKER] 測試矩陣沒有覆蓋計畫自己定義的錯誤契約。至少缺：非 JSON Content-Type、壞 JSON、非字串／空字串、缺 Airtable key、LINE upstream failure、Airtable GET network/404/non-ok/bad JSON、PATCH network/non-ok，以及 `/history`、`/log` 新欄位回傳。
3130:
3131:- [BLOCKER] 現有回歸鉤可假綠燈。我以不存在的 `WORKER_JS_PATH` 實測，Worker V1 被標示 `SKIP`，整支測試仍以 `EXIT_CODE=0` 結束。必要 artifact 缺失必須 FAIL；Node import／執行異常也須直接傳遞非零狀態，並檢查最低測試數。
3132:
3133:- [BLOCKER] 前端顯示使用者輸入，但計畫未規定 HTML escaping。若經 `innerHTML` 插入，會形成 stored XSS。須明定只用 `textContent` 或既有 escaping helper，並以 `<img onerror=...>`、`<script>`、引號及換行加入回歸案例。
3134:
3135:- [BLOCKER] log.html 驗收方法無法證明互動正確。字串／regex 只能證明程式碼存在，不能證明「已有／沒有 trace」、「成功轉態」及「失敗保留入口」真的互斥。須執行 DOM 函式或 browser-level test；Cloudflare端亦建議用 Workers Vitest integration 捕捉 Node mock 漏掉的 runtime 差異。[Cloudflare Workers 測試文件](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/testing/vitest-integration/)
3136:
3137:- [SUGGEST] Content-Type 不應以寬鬆 `includes("application/json")` 判斷，否則 `application/jsonp` 也可能通過。應解析 MIME，允許 `application/json` 與合法 charset 參數。
3138:
3139:- [SUGGEST] 「500 字」需定義計數單位。JS `.length`／textarea `maxlength` 計 UTF-16 code units，emoji 可能算兩個；應明定 code units、Unicode code points 或 grapheme clusters，並測試 500／501 邊界。
3140:
3141:- [SUGGEST] 為 LINE／Airtable fetch 加 timeout；Airtable `429` 建議保留 `Retry-After` 或映射成可重試的 503，而非全部壓成 502。
3142:
3143:- [SUGGEST] OPTIONS 測試不能只驗 204；應同時斷言 `Access-Control-Allow-Origin`、Methods、Headers，並測允許來源。
3144:
3145:- [SUGGEST] `/history`、`/log` 會回傳 TA 自由文字，建議加 `Cache-Control: no-store`，避免敏感內容被瀏覽器或代理快取。
3146:
3147:- [SUGGEST] `grep -c` 只計算「含任一文案的行數」，不能保證五條各一次；應逐字串分別計數並讓任何不等於 1 的結果退出非零。

3197:name: workers-best-practices
3198:description: Reviews and authors Cloudflare Workers code against production best practices.
